Fallback: just return digits with hyphens every 3-4 chars\n        return digits\n\n\ndef build_replacement_dict(data):\n    \"\"\"Build mapping from placeholders to final values.\"\"\"\n    # Compute age\n    age = compute_korean_age(data['생년월일'], data['방문일'])\n\n    # Track which paragraphs were modified\n    modified_paras = set()\n\n    # Walk all elements\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        # Check text content\n        if elem.text and '{{' in elem.text:\n            new_text = replace_in_text(elem.text, replacements)\n            if new_text != elem.text:\n                elem.text = new_text\n                # Mark parent paragraph as modified\n                parent = find_parent(root, elem)\n                while parent is not None:\n                    if parent.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/schema/2011/hp/}p':\n                        modified_paras.add(parent)\n                        break\n                    parent = find_parent(root, parent)\n\n        # Mark parent paragraph as modified\n                parent = find_parent(root, elem)\n                while parent is not None:\n                    if parent.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/schema/2011/hp/}p':\n                        modified_paras.add(parent)\n                        break\n                    parent = find_parent(root, parent)\n\n    #
